<svg class="icon"><use xlink:href="/resources/prod/a1fade881/i/svg-output/icons.svg#icn-location-pin" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink"></use></svg>
                            Bristol
                        </span>
<h5 class="venue-card-cwc__title">Bristol County Ground</h5>
<p class="venue-card-cwc__description">Home to Gloucestershire CCC and venue for three CWC19 games.
CWC moment: Sachin Tendulkar smashed 140* off 101 balls v Kenya in 1999</p>
</figcaption>
</figure>
</a>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
